<h1 id="노트북-lm-기반-c-side-공부">노트북 LM 기반 C-SIDE 공부</h1>
<h1 id="요약">1 요약</h1>
<p>제시된 소스들을 바탕으로 <strong>C-SIDE(Cell type-Specific Inference of Differential Expression)</strong> 논문의 핵심 내용을 요약해 드립니다.</p>
<h3 id="c-side의-개요-및-목적">1. C-SIDE의 개요 및 목적</h3>
<p><strong>C-SIDE</strong>는 공간 전사체(Spatial Transcriptomics) 데이터에서 <strong>특정 세포 유형 내에서의 차등 발현(Differential Expression, DE)</strong> 유전자를 탐지하기 위해 개발된 일반 모수적 통계 방법입니다. 이 기술의 핵심 목적은 공간적 위치, 병리적 특징과의 인접성, 또는 세포 간 상호작용과 같은 다양한 **조직 맥락(Tissue Context)**에 따라 세포의 유전자 발현이 어떻게 변화하는지 정확하게 추정하는 것입니다.</p>
<h3 id="주요-해결-과제-세포-혼합cell-type-mixtures-문제">2. 주요 해결 과제: 세포 혼합(Cell Type Mixtures) 문제</h3>
<p>기존의 공간 전사체 분석법들이 직면한 가장 큰 문제는 하나의 측정 단위(픽셀 또는 스팟) 내에 <strong>여러 세포 유형이 섞여 있을 수 있다는 점</strong>입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>편향 발생:</strong> 세포 유형의 비율 변화를 고려하지 않고 DE를 분석하면, 실제 발현 변화가 아닌 세포 구성의 차이로 인해 잘못된 결과(편향)가 도출될 수 있습니다.</li>
<li><strong>C-SIDE의 해결책:</strong> C-SIDE는 각 픽셀의 세포 유형 비율을 먼저 추정한 후, 이를 모델에 반영하여 특정 세포 유형에서 발생하는 고유한 발현 변화만을 분리해 냅니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="분석-알고리즘-및-작동-원리">3. 분석 알고리즘 및 작동 원리</h3>
<p>C-SIDE의 분석 과정은 크게 세 단계로 나뉩니다:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>세포 유형 비율 추정:</strong> 단일 세포 RNA-seq(scRNA-seq) 참조 데이터를 활용해 각 픽셀에 포함된 세포 유형별 비율을 계산합니다.</li>
<li><strong>모수적 모델 피팅:</strong> 정의된 공변량(Covariates, 예: 거리, 지역 등)을 사용하여 유전자 발현을 모델링합니다. 이때 로그 선형(Log-linear) 모델을 사용하여 세포 유형별 DE 추정치와 표준 오차를 구합니다.</li>
<li><strong>통계적 추론:</strong> 샘플 간의 기술적/생물학적 변이를 고려하여 여러 반복 실험(Replicates) 데이터를 통합하고, 최종적으로 통계적으로 유의미한 유전자를 식별합니다.</li>
</ol>
<h3 id="다양한-활용-시나리오">4. 다양한 활용 시나리오</h3>
<p>C-SIDE는 매우 유연한 프레임워크를 가지고 있어 다음과 같은 다양한 연구에 적용될 수 있습니다:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>해부학적 지역 간 비교:</strong> 조직 내 서로 다른 영역(예: 소뇌의 전엽 vs 결절) 사이의 발현 차이 분석.</li>
<li><strong>거리 기반 분석:</strong> 특정 지표(예: 시상하부 중앙선)로부터의 거리에 따른 변화 분석.</li>
<li><strong>병리적 특징과의 관계:</strong> 알츠하이머병의 <strong>아밀로이드 베타 플라크(Aβ plaque)</strong> 밀도에 따른 면역 세포의 반응 분석.</li>
<li><strong>세포 간 상호작용:</strong> 특정 세포 유형이 인접해 있을 때 발생하는 발현 변화 분석.</li>
<li><strong>비모수적 분석:</strong> 구체적인 가설 없이도 조직 전체의 일반적인 공간 발현 패턴을 탐색 가능.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="소프트웨어-구현">5. 소프트웨어 구현</h3>
<p>이 알고리즘은 R 패키지인 **<code>spacexr</code>**에 포함되어 배포되고 있으며, 공간 RNA 데이터 처리를 위한 <code>SpatialRNA</code> 객체와 참조 데이터를 위한 <code>Reference</code> 객체 등을 사용하여 분석을 수행합니다.</p>
<p>요약하자면, C-SIDE는 공간 전사체 데이터에서 <strong>세포 혼합의 한계를 극복</strong>하고, <strong>다양한 생물학적 요인에 따른 세포 유형별 발현 변화를 정밀하게 분석</strong>할 수 있게 해주는 혁신적인 도구입니다.</p>
<p>더 구체적으로 알고 싶은 부분(예: 수학적 모델링, 특정 실험 결과 등)이 있다면 말씀해 주세요!</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="강의용-통계-모델-설명-슬라이드">2 강의용 통계 모델 설명 슬라이드</h1>
<p>대학원생 강의용으로 적합하도록 <strong>C-SIDE의 통계적 모델링</strong> 핵심 내용을 중심으로 구성한 발표 슬라이드 초안입니다. 각 슬라이드는 핵심 개념, 수학적 정의, 그리고 분석 절차를 포함하고 있습니다.</p>
<hr>
<h3 id="강의-제목-c-side의-통계적-프레임워크와-공간-차등-발현-분석"><strong>[강의 제목: C-SIDE의 통계적 프레임워크와 공간 차등 발현 분석]</strong></h3>
<h4 id="slide-1-공간-전사체-분석의-핵심-과제와-c-side의-목적"><strong>Slide 1: 공간 전사체 분석의 핵심 과제와 C-SIDE의 목적</strong></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong>핵심 문제 (Motivation):</strong> 공간 전사체(ST) 데이터의 픽셀(Pixel)은 여러 세포 유형이 섞여 있는 **세포 혼합물(Cell type mixtures)**인 경우가 많습니다.</li>
<li><strong>문제점:</strong> 세포 구성 비율의 변화를 고려하지 않으면, 실제 유전자 발현 변화가 아닌 세포 비율의 차이를 발현 변화로 오인하는 편향(Bias)이 발생합니다.</li>
<li><strong>C-SIDE의 해결책:</strong> 각 픽셀의 세포 유형 비율을 모델에 통합하여, **특정 세포 유형 내에서의 차등 발현(Cell type-specific DE)**을 통계적으로 추정합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="slide-2-계층적-확률-모델-hierarchical-probabilistic-model---1"><strong>Slide 2: 계층적 확률 모델 (Hierarchical Probabilistic Model) - (1)</strong></h4>
<ul>

<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 기대되는 유전자 발현율</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>가산적 혼합 모델 (Additive Mixture Model):</strong> 기대 발현율은 각 세포 유형의 기여도를 합산한 형태로 정의됩니다.
<ul>

margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>주요 변수 정의:</strong>
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_{i,l,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 연구자가 정의한 공변량 (예: 특정 영역 여부, 특정 지점으로부터의 거리, 병리적 밀도 등)</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{l,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: <strong>우리가 구하고자 하는 핵심 계수.</strong> 공변량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span></span>에 따른 세포 유형 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>의 DE 효과 크기(Log fold-change)를 나타냅니다.</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{0,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 절편(Intercept) 항.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="slide-4-최대-우도-추정-maximum-likelihood-estimation-mle"><strong>Slide 4: 최대 우도 추정 (Maximum Likelihood Estimation, MLE)</strong></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong>분석 단계:</strong>
<ol>
<li><strong>세포 비율 추정:</strong> RCTD 알고리즘을 사용하여 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta_{i,k,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05278em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>와 플랫폼 효과 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma_{j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05556em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>를 먼저 추정합니다.</li>
<li><strong>과분산 추정:</strong> 유전자별 과분산 매개변수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>를 추정합니다.</li>
<li><strong>DE 계수 추정:</strong> 고정된 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>σ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta, \gamma, \sigma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span></span></span></span></span> 값을 바탕으로 <strong>2차 신뢰 영역 최적화(Second-order trust-region optimization)</strong> 알고리즘을 사용하여 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 산출합니다.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>최적화 특징:</strong> 각 유전자별 우도(Likelihood)가 독립적이므로 대규모 데이터에 대해 병렬 처리가 가능합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="slide-5-통계적-추론-및-가설-검정-statistical-inference"><strong>Slide 5: 통계적 추론 및 가설 검정 (Statistical Inference)</strong></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong>점근적 정규성 (Asymptotic Normality):</strong> 픽셀 수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span></span></span></span></span>이 충분히 클 때, 추정된 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>는 정규 분포를 따릅니다.
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>−</mo><mi>α</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>≈</mo><mi>N</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><msubsup><mi>I</mi><mi>α</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sqrt{n}(\hat{\alpha} - \alpha) \approx N(0, I^{-1}_{\alpha})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.05028em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord sqrt"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.80028em;"><span class="svg-align" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord" style="padding-left: 0.833em;"><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -2.76028em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="hide-tail" style="min-width: 0.853em; height: 1.08em;"><svg width="400em" height="1.08em" viewBox="0 0 400000 1080" preserveAspectRatio="xMinYMin slice"><path d="M95,702
c-2.7,0,-7.17,-2.7,-13.5,-8c-5.8,-5.3,-9.5,-10,-9.5,-14
c0,-2,0.3,-3.3,1,-4c1.3,-2.7,23.83,-20.7,67.5,-54
c44.2,-33.3,65.8,-50.3,66.5,-51c1.3,-1.3,3,-2,5,-2c4.7,0,8.7,3.3,12,10
s173,378,173,378c0.7,0,35.3,-71,104,-213c68.7,-142,137.5,-285,206.5,-429
c69,-144,104.5,-217.7,106.5,-221
l0 -0
c5.3,-9.3,12,-14,20,-14
H400000v40H845.2724
s-225.272,467,-225.272,467s-235,486,-235,486c-2.7,4.7,-9,7,-19,7
c-6,0,-10,-1,-12,-3s-194,-422,-194,-422s-65,47,-65,47z
M834 80h400000v40h-400000z"></path></svg></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.23972em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.222222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.222222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">≈</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.06411em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.07847em;">I</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.453em; margin-left: -0.07847em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">−</span><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.247em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span> (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>I</mi><mi>α</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">I_{\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.83333em; vertical-align: -0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.07847em;">I</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.07847em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>는 피셔 정보 행렬)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>가설 검정:</strong>
<ul>
<li>표준 오차(Standard Error) <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span></span>를 계산하고, <strong>Z-test</strong>를 통해 유의 확률(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">P</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.13889em;">P</span></span></span></span></span>-value)을 도출합니다.</li>
<li>Benjamini-Hochberg 절차를 사용하여 다중 가설 검정(FDR)을 수행합니다.</li>
<li>기본적으로 FDR 0.01 및 Fold-change 1.5배 이상을 유의미한 유전자로 판단합니다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h4 id="slide-6-다중-반복-실험-및-샘플-통합-inference-across-replicates"><strong>Slide 6: 다중 반복 실험 및 샘플 통합 (Inference across Replicates)</strong></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong>계층적 분산 모델:</strong> 개별 샘플의 추정치 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>g</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}_g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>가 인구 집단 평균 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span>를 중심으로 분포한다고 가정합니다.
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mi>g</mi></msub><mo>∼</mo><mi>N</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_g \sim N(A, \tau^2)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.06411em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span> (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span></span></span></span></span>: 샘플 간 생물학적/기술적 변이)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>통합 알고리즘:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>Method of Moments</strong>를 통해 샘플 간 분산 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau^2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.814108em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>을 추정합니다.</li>
<li><strong>가중 평균(Weighted average):</strong> 각 샘플의 표준 오차와 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau^2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.814108em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>을 고려하여 인구 집단 수준의 DE(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span>)를 산출합니다 (Meta-analysis 방식과 유사).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>장점:</strong> 단일 샘플에서 발생할 수 있는 거짓 양성(False discovery)을 줄이고 보다 강건한(Robust) 결과를 도출합니다.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<p><strong>참고 사항:</strong> 이 슬라이드 구성은 C-SIDE의 수학적 정밀함과 생물학적 해석 가능성을 동시에 강조하고 있습니다. 강의 시에는 <strong>Slide 2의 가산적 모델</strong>이 어떻게 혼합된 시그널을 분리해내는지 시각적으로 설명하면 대학원생들이 더 쉽게 이해할 수 있을 것입니다.</p>
<p>추가로 특정 알고리즘의 R 구현(예: <code>run.CSIDE.single</code> 함수 등)에 대한 실습 자료가 필요하시면 말씀해 주세요.</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="통계-모델-설명">3 통계 모델 설명</h1>
<p>네, 가능합니다. 요청하신 대로 <strong>C-SIDE(Cell type-Specific Inference of Differential Expression)</strong> 논문의 핵심 내용과 수식을 포함한 통계 모델의 상세 설명을 Markdown 형식으로 작성해 드립니다.</p>
<hr>
<h2 id="c-side-공간-전사체-데이터를-위한-세포-유형별-차등-발현-분석-모델">C-SIDE: 공간 전사체 데이터를 위한 세포 유형별 차등 발현 분석 모델</h2>
<h2 id="개요-및-목적">1. 개요 및 목적</h2>
<p>C-SIDE는 공간 전사체(Spatial Transcriptomics) 데이터에서 하나의 픽셀(Pixel) 내에 여러 세포 유형이 섞여 있는 <strong>세포 혼합(Cell type mixtures)</strong> 문제를 해결하기 위해 설계되었습니다. 이 모델의 목적은 특정 세포 유형 내에서 공간적 위치나 병리적 특성(예: 아밀로이드 플라크 밀도)과 같은 **공변량(Covariates)**에 따른 유전자 발현 변화를 통계적으로 정확하게 추정하는 것입니다.</p>
<h2 id="계층적-확률-모델-hierarchical-probabilistic-model">2. 계층적 확률 모델 (Hierarchical Probabilistic Model)</h2>
<p>C-SIDE는 관측된 RNA 카운트 데이터를 모델링하기 위해 포아송 분포와 로그 선형 모델을 결합한 계층적 구조를 사용합니다.</p>
<h3 id="관측-모델-likelihood">2.1 관측 모델 (Likelihood)</h3>
<p>샘플 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span>의 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>에서 유전자 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>의 관측된 카운트 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>는 다음과 같이 포아송 분포를 따른다고 가정합니다:</p>
<p><span class="katex--display"><span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>∼</mo><mtext>Poisson</mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>N</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g} | \lambda_{i,j,g} \sim \text{Poisson}(N_{i,g}\lambda_{i,j,g})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord text"><span class="mord">Poisson</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.10903em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span></span></p>
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>N</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N_{i,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.10903em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 해당 픽셀의 총 전사체 수 (Library size).</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 기대되는 유전자 발현율.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="가산적-혼합-모델-additive-mixture-model">2.2 가산적 혼합 모델 (Additive Mixture Model)</h3>
<p>기대 발현율 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>는 각 세포 유형의 기여도를 합산하여 모델링하며, 로그 공간에서 다음과 같이 정의됩니다:</p>
<p><span class="katex--display"><span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo fence="true">(</mo><munderover><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>K</mi></munderover><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo fence="true">)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>ϵ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log(\lambda_{i,j,g}) = \log\left(\sum_{k=1}^K \beta_{i,k,g}\mu_{i,k,j,g}\right) + \gamma_{j,g} + \epsilon_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right: 0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; <ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta_{i,k,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; margin-left: -0.05278em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>의 비율 (RCTD를 통해 사전 추정됨).</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>의 세포 유형 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>에서 유전자 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>의 기대 발현량.</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma_{j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05556em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 유전자별 플랫폼 효과 (Platform random effect).</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>ϵ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">ϵ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 기술적/생물학적 변이를 반영하는 유전자별 과분산(Overdispersion) 항.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="차등-발현의-매개변수화-parametrization-of-de">3. 차등 발현의 매개변수화 (Parametrization of DE)</h2>
<p>C-SIDE의 핵심은 세포 유형별 발현량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>를 공변량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>의 함수로 표현하는 것입니다:</p>
<p><span class="katex--display"><span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><munderover><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>L</mi></munderover><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log(\mu_{i,k,j,g}) = \alpha_{0,k,j,g} + \sum_{l=1}^L x_{i,l,g}\alpha_{l,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right: 0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.869438em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_{i,l,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span></span>번째 공변량 (예: 좌표, 특정 영역과의 거리 등).</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{l,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: <strong>차등 발현 계수.</strong> 공변량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span></span>에 따른 세포 유형 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>의 발현 변화량(Log fold-change)을 나타냅니다.</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{0,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 절편(Intercept) 항.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="모델-추정-및-최적화">4. 모델 추정 및 최적화</h2>
<h3 id="단계별-추정-과정">4.1 단계별 추정 과정</h3>
<ol>
<li><strong>사전 단계:</strong> RCTD 알고리즘을 사용하여 세포 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>)과 플랫폼 효과(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>)를 추정합니다.</li>
<li><strong>과분산 추정:</strong> 유전자별 과분산 매개변수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>를 최대 우도 추정법(MLE)으로 계산합니다.</li>
<li><strong>핵심 단계:</strong> 고정된 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>σ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta, \gamma, \sigma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span></span></span></span></span> 값을 바탕으로 DE 계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 추정합니다.</li>
</ol>
<h3 id="신뢰-영역-최적화-trust-region-optimization">4.2 신뢰 영역 최적화 (Trust-region Optimization)</h3>
<p>우도 함수(Likelihood function)를 최적화하기 위해 C-SIDE는 <strong>2차 신뢰 영역 기반 최적화</strong> 알고리즘을 사용합니다. 이 방식은 로그 우도의 2차 테일러 근사(Quadratic Taylor approximation)를 반복적으로 풀면서 매 단계마다 매개변수의 변화량을 적응적으로 제한하여 안정적인 수렴을 보장합니다.</p>
<h2 id="통계적-추론-statistical-inference">5. 통계적 추론 (Statistical Inference)</h2>
<h3 id="점근적-정규성-및-가설-검정">5.1 점근적 정규성 및 가설 검정</h3>
<p>픽셀 수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span></span></span></span></span>이 클 때, MLE 추정치 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>는 점근적으로 정규 분포를 따릅니다: <span class="katex--display"><span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mrow><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>−</mo><mi>α</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>∼</mo><mi>N</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><msubsup><mi>I</mi><mi>α</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sqrt{n}(\hat{\alpha} - \alpha) \sim N(0, I_{\alpha}^{-1})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.09916em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord sqrt"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.849155em;"><span class="svg-align" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord" style="padding-left: 0.833em;"><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -2.80916em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="hide-tail" style="min-width: 0.853em;
