vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.11411em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.07847em;">I</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.864108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.453em; margin-left: -0.07847em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -3.113em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">−</span><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.247em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span></span> 여기서 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>I</mi><mi>α</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">I_{\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.83333em; vertical-align: -0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.07847em;">I</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.07847em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>는 피셔 정보 행렬(Fisher Information Matrix)입니다. 이를 통해 표준 오차를 계산하고, <strong>Z-test</strong>를 수행하여 각 유전자의 유의 확률(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">P</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.13889em;">P</span></span></span></span></span>-value)을 도출합니다.</p>
<h3 id="반복-실험-통합-inference-across-replicates">5.2 반복 실험 통합 (Inference across Replicates)</h3>
<p>여러 샘플 또는 반복 실험 데이터가 있는 경우, 개별 샘플의 추정치 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>g</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}_g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>를 통합하여 인구 집단 수준의 효과 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span>를 산출합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>모델:</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mi>g</mi></msub><mo>∼</mo><mi>N</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_g \sim N(A, \tau^2)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.06411em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span> (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span></span></span></span></span>: 샘플 간 변이).</li>
<li><strong>통합 방식:</strong> 가중 평균을 사용하는 <strong>가중 최소 제곱법(Weighted Least Squares)</strong> 방식을 적용하여 보다 강건한 통계적 결론을 내립니다.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<p>이 내용은 소스 자료의 수식과 설명을 직접 인용하여 작성되었습니다. 추가적인 세부 수식이나 특정 데이터셋 적용 사례가 필요하시면 알려주세요.</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="계층적-확률-모델">4 계층적 확률 모델</h1>
<p>레퍼런스 18(RCTD 논문)과 62(DerSimonian-Laird 메타 분석)가 추가됨에 따라, C-SIDE의 **계층적 확률 모델(Hierarchical Probabilistic Model)**을 가장 낮은 데이터 관측 단계부터 가장 높은 인구 집단 추론 단계까지 통합적으로 설명할 수 있게 되었습니다.</p>
<p>C-SIDE의 모델은 크게 4가지 계층으로 구성됩니다.</p>
<h3 id="단계-픽셀-단위-관측-모델-poisson-sampling">1단계: 픽셀 단위 관측 모델 (Poisson Sampling)</h3>
<p>가장 기본 계층은 공간 전사체 데이터의 특성인 ‘카운트(Count)’ 데이터를 모델링하는 것입니다. 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>, 유전자 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>, 샘플 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span>에서의 관측된 RNA 카운트 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>는 <strong>포아송 분포</strong>를 따른다고 가정합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>수식:</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>∼</mo><mtext>Poisson</mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>N</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g} | \lambda_{i,j,g} \sim \text{Poisson}(N_{i,g}\lambda_{i,j,g})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord text"><span class="mord">Poisson</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.10903em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span></li>
<li><strong>의미:</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span></span></span></span></span>은 픽셀의 총 전사체 수(Library size)이며, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>는 해당 유전자가 발현될 기대 확률입니다. 이 포아송 모델링을 통해 전사체 수가 적은 데이터(예: Slide-seq)에서 발생하는 샘플링 노이즈를 효과적으로 처리합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-가산적-혼합-모델-additive-mixture-model">2단계: 가산적 혼합 모델 (Additive Mixture Model)</h3>
<p>두 번째 계층은 픽셀 내의 <strong>세포 혼합물</strong>을 분해하는 단계로, **레퍼런스 18(RCTD)**의 핵심 알고리즘이 적용됩니다.</p>

<li><strong>주요 변수 (Ref 18 기반):</strong>
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta_{i,k,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; margin-left: -0.05278em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span> 내 세포 유형 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>의 비율입니다. RCTD는 scRNA-seq 참조 데이터를 사용해 이 비율을 미리 추정합니다.</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma_{j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05556em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: **플랫폼 효과(Platform Effect)**입니다. 시퀀싱 기술 간의 체계적 차이를 보정하는 무작위 효과입니다.</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>ϵ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">ϵ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>: 유전자별 **과분산(Overdispersion)**을 반영하는 항으로, 공간적 변이나 생물학적 변이를 흡수합니다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-세포-유형별-de-매개변수화-log-linear-model">3단계: 세포 유형별 DE 매개변수화 (Log-linear Model)</h3>
<p>세 번째 계층은 C-SIDE만의 핵심 기여분으로, 각 세포 유형의 고유 발현량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>를 **공변량(Covariates)**의 함수로 정의하는 단계입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>수식:</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>L</mi></msubsup><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log(\mu_{i,k,j,g}) = \alpha_{0,k,j,g} + \sum_{l=1}^L x_{i,l,g}\alpha_{l,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; <li><strong>의미:</strong>
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>: 연구자가 정의한 공간적 위치, 거리, 병리적 밀도 등의 공변량입니다.</li>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>: 우리가 찾고자 하는 <strong>차등 발현 계수</strong>입니다. 특정 세포 유형 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span> 내에서 공변량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>에 의해 유전자 발현이 얼마나 변하는지(Log fold-change)를 나타냅니다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-다중-반복-실험-통합-meta-analysis-approach">4단계: 다중 반복 실험 통합 (Meta-Analysis Approach)</h3>
<p>마지막 계층은 여러 샘플(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span>)에서 얻은 결과를 통합하여 인구 집단 수준의 결론을 도출하는 단계입니다. 이 과정에서 **레퍼런스 62(DerSimonian-Laird)**의 메타 분석 기법이 사용됩니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>계층적 분산 모델:</strong> 각 샘플의 DE 추정치 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>g</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}_g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>가 인구 집단 평균 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span>를 중심으로 정규 분포한다고 가정합니다.
<ul>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mi>g</mi></msub><mo>∼</mo><mi>N</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_g \sim N(A, \tau^2)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.06411em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span> (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span></span></span></span></span>: 샘플 간 생물학적/기술적 변이)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>추론 방식 (Ref 62 기반):</strong> <strong>Method of Moments</strong>를 사용하여 샘플 간 분산 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau^2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.814108em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>을 추정하고, 이를 바탕으로 각 샘플 결과에 가중치를 부여해 통합 평균 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span>를 계산합니다. 이 방식은 단일 샘플에서 발생할 수 있는 가짜 양성(False discovery)을 줄이고 결과의 강건성을 보장합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="요약-1">요약</h3>
<p>이 계층적 모델을 통해 C-SIDE는 **“포아송 샘플링(1단계) → 세포 혼합 해소(2단계, Ref 18) → 세포별 발현 변화 추정(3단계) → 샘플 간 통합(4단계, Ref 62)”**의 과정을 거쳐, 복잡한 조직 환경 속에서 특정 세포 유형이 어떻게 반응하는지를 정밀하게 계산해 낼 수 있습니다.</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="results--c-side-model">5 Results : C-SIDE Model</h1>
<p>C-SIDE 논문의 <strong>Results</strong> 섹션에서 해당 문단은 모델의 전체적인 구조와 수학적 설계를 요약하고 있습니다. 요청하신 문구('Here, we develop C-SIDE…'부터 '… for gene j in cell type k for sample g’까지)를 문장 단위로 상세히 설명해 드립니다.</p>
<h3 id="c-side의-개발-목적"><strong>1. C-SIDE의 개발 목적</strong></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>원문:</strong> “Here, we develop C-SIDE, a statistical method for determining DE in spatial transcriptomics datasets (Fig. 1a).”</li>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 이 문장은 C-SIDE가 공간 전사체 데이터셋에서 **차등 발현(Differential Expression, DE)**을 결정하기 위해 개발된 <strong>통계적 방법</strong>임을 정의하며 시작합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="입력-데이터-정의"><strong>2. 입력 데이터 정의</strong></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>원문:</strong> “C-SIDE inputs one or more experimental samples of spatial transcriptomics data, consisting of <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> as the observed RNA counts for pixel <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>, gene <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span> and experimental sample <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span>.”</li>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> C-SIDE는 하나 이상의 실험 샘플을 입력으로 받습니다. 여기서 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>는 실험 샘플 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span>의 특정 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>에서 관측된 특정 유전자 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>의 <strong>RNA 카운트(발현량) 데이터</strong>를 의미합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="관측-모델-poisson-sampling"><strong>3. 관측 모델 (Poisson Sampling)</strong></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>원문:</strong> “We then assume Poisson sampling so that <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>≈</mo><mtext>Poisson</mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>N</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g} | \lambda_{i,j,g} \approx \text{Poisson}(N_{i,g}\lambda_{i,j,g})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">≈</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span> on experimental sample <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; <li><strong>설명:</strong> 관측된 데이터 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>가 <strong>포아송 분포</strong>를 따른다고 가정하는 단계입니다. <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span></span></span></span></span>은 해당 픽셀의 **총 전사체 수(Library size)**를 의미하며, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>는 해당 유전자의 <strong>기대 발현율</strong>을 나타냅니다. 이 모델링은 Slide-seq과 같이 전사체 수가 적은 데이터에서 발생하는 샘플링 노이즈를 처리하는 데 유용합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="가산적-혼합-모델-additive-mixture-model-1"><strong>4. 가산적 혼합 모델 (Additive Mixture Model)</strong></h3>

vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>를 <strong>세포 유형들의 혼합물</strong>로 모델링합니다. 로그 공간에서 정의된 이 수식은 픽셀 내에 섞여 있는 여러 세포 유형(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>K</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">K</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.07153em;">K</span></span></span></span></span>)의 기여도를 합산한 형태입니다. 여기에는 기술적 차이를 보정하는 **플랫폼 효과(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>)**와 생물학적/공간적 변이를 반영하는 **과분산 항(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">ϵ</span></span></span></span></span>)**이 포함됩니다.</li>
</ul>

margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> a random effect to account for gene-specific overdispersion.”</li>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 위 수식에 사용된 변수들을 정의합니다. **<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>**는 특정 세포 유형 내에서의 유전자 발현율, **<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>**는 해당 픽셀에서 그 세포 유형이 차지하는 비율, **<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>**는 플랫폼 간 차이를 보정하는 무작위 효과, **<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">ϵ</span></span></span></span></span>**은 유전자별 과분산을 의미합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="세포-유형별-차등-발현de의-모델링"><strong>6. 세포 유형별 차등 발현(DE)의 모델링</strong></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>원문:</strong> “To account for cell type-specific DE, we model across pixel locations the log of the cell type-specific profiles <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> as a linear combination of <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">L</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">L</span></span></span></span></span> covariates used to explain DE.”</li>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> C-SIDE의 핵심 기여 부분으로, 세포 유형별 고유 발현량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>가 픽셀 위치에 따라 어떻게 변하는지 모델링합니다. 이를 위해 유전자 발현 변화를 설명할 수 있는 **<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">L</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">L</span></span></span></span></span>개의 공변량(Covariates)**의 <strong>선형 결합</strong>으로 표현합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="로그-선형-매개변수화-log-linear-model"><strong>7. 로그 선형 매개변수화 (Log-linear Model)</strong></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>원문:</strong> “Specifically, we assume that, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>L</mi></msubsup><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log(\mu_{i,k,j,g}) = \alpha_{0,k,j,g} + \sum_{\ell=1}^L x_{i,\ell,g}\alpha_{\ell,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right: 0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.869438em; vertical-align: -0.29971em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mop op-symbol small-op" style="position: relative; top: -5e-06em;">∑</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.981231em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.40029em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mrel mtight">=</span><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -3.2029em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>.”</li>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 실제 계산에 사용되는 수식(식 3)입니다. 유전자 발현량(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)의 로그값이 절편(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.58056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>)들의 합으로 결정된다는 <strong>로그 선형 모델</strong>을 정의합니다.</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="계수-alpha의-통계적-의미"><strong>9. 계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>의 통계적 의미</strong></h3>

margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> each represent the DE effect size of covariate <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ell</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span> in cell type <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>)은 차등 발현을 설명하는 요인들을 담고 있습니다. 우리가 최종적으로 구하고자 하는 **계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord">ℓ</span></span></span></span></span>)이 특정 유전자(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>)와 세포 유형(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>)에 미치는 **효과 크기(Effect size, 즉 발현 변화 정도)**를 나타냅니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="요약-2"><strong>요약</strong></h3>
<p>이 문단은 C-SIDE가 <strong>(1) 포아송 샘플링</strong>을 통해 기초 데이터를 읽고, <strong>(2) 세포 혼합 모델</strong>을 통해 픽셀 내 시그널을 분해하며, <strong>(3) 로그 선형 모델</strong>을 통해 특정 세포 유형이 주변 환경(공변량)에 따라 유전자 발현을 얼마나 바꾸는지(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>)를 통계적으로 계산해 내는 전체적인 논리를 설명하고 있습니다.</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="methods--c-side-model">6 Methods : C-SIDE Model</h1>
<p>C-SIDE 논문의 <strong>Methods</strong> 섹션에서 요청하신 두 문단(본문,번 구절)은 모델의 수학적 정의와 설계 철학을 담고 있는 핵심적인 부분입니다. 이를 문장별로 상세히 설명해 드립니다.</p>
<h3 id="첫-번째-문단-c-side-모델의-구성-요소-및-변수-정의"><strong>첫 번째 문단: C-SIDE 모델의 구성 요소 및 변수 정의</strong></h3>
<ol>
<li>
<p><strong>"Here, we describe C-SIDE, a statistical method for identifying DE in spatial transcriptomics data."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> C-SIDE가 공간 전사체 데이터에서 <strong>차등 발현(Differential Expression, DE)</strong> 유전자를 식별하기 위한 통계적 방법임을 선언하며 시작합니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"We define the C-SIDE model in equations (1)–(3)."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 모델의 기초가 되는 세 가지 수식을 참조합니다. 이는 <strong>포아송 샘플링(식 1)</strong>, <strong>가산적 혼합 모델(식 2)</strong>, 그리고 **로그 선형 차등 발현 매개변수화(식 3)**를 의미합니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"Before fitting C-SIDE, the design matrix is predefined to contain covariates, variables on which gene expression is hypothesized to depend such as spatial position or cellular microenvironment."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 분석 전, 연구자는 유전자 발현에 영향을 줄 것으로 예상되는 <strong>공변량(Covariates)</strong>(예: 좌표, 특정 세포와의 거리, 병리적 환경 등)을 포함하는 **설계 행렬(Design Matrix, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>)**을 미리 정의해야 합니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"Recall that <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_{i,l,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; <ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 변수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_{i,l,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; </ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"For each covariate <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mo>⋅</mo><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>l</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_{\cdot,l,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; <ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 각 공변량에는 그에 대응하는 **계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>**가 존재합니다. 이 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span> 내에서 공변량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>의 발현이 얼마나 변하는지(Log fold-change)를 나타내는 핵심 추정치입니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"Next, recall from equation (2) random effects <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma_{j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; margin-left: -0.05556em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> and <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>ϵ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> and <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; <ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 모델에는 두 가지 무작위 효과가 포함됩니다. <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>는 플랫폼 효과(참조 데이터와 공간 데이터 간의 기술적 차이)를, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">ϵ</span></span></span></span></span>은 과분산(자연적인 변이)을 반영하며, 둘 다 평균이 0인 정규 분포를 따른다고 가정합니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"The overdispersion magnitude, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> varies across gene <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span> (Supplementary Fig. 12) and modeling gene-specific overdispersion is necessary for controlling the false-positive rate of C-SIDE."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> **과분산의 크기(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.58056em; vertical-align: -0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>)**는 유전자마다 다릅니다. 따라서 각 유전자에 최적화된 과분산 값을 모델링하는 것이 가짜 양성(False-positive) 결과를 억제하고 통계적 정확도를 높이는 데 필수적입니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<hr>
<h3 id="두-번째-문단-세포-유형별-특이성-및-glm과의-관계"><strong>두 번째 문단: 세포 유형별 특이성 및 GLM과의 관계</strong></h3>
<ol>
<li>
<p><strong>"Due to our finding that genes can exhibit DE in some but not all cell types (Fig. 3c), C-SIDE generally does not assume that genes share DE patterns across cell types, allowing for the discovery of cell type-specific DE."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 연구 결과, 특정 유전자가 일부 세포 유형에서만 차등 발현될 수 있음이 확인되었습니다(예: <em>Aldoc</em> 유전자는 푸르키니예 세포에서만 변함). 따라서 C-SIDE는 모든 세포가 동일하게 반응한다고 가정하지 않고, <strong>각 세포 유형별로 고유한 발현 변화</strong>를 찾아낼 수 있게 설계되었습니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"We also developed an option where DE can be assumed to be shared across cell types (Supplementary Notes)."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 하지만 모든 세포 유형이 특정 환경에 공통적으로 반응할 것으로 예상되는 경우를 위해, 차등 발현 패턴을 세포 유형 간에 공유하도록 설정하는 옵션도 제공합니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"C-SIDE can be thought of as a modification of the generalized linear model (GLM) in which each cell type follows a cell type-specific log-linear model before an additive mixture of all cell types is observed."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> C-SIDE는 통계학의 **일반화 선형 모델(GLM)**을 변형한 형태로 이해할 수 있습니다. 핵심은 각 세포 유형이 각자의 로그 선형 모델을 따르고, 우리가 실제로 관측하는 픽셀 데이터는 이들이 **가산적으로 혼합(Additive mixture)**된 결과물이라는 점입니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>"See Fitting the C-SIDE model and Hypothesis testing for C-SIDE model fitting and hypothesis testing, respectively."</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>설명:</strong> 모델을 실제로 데이터에 적합시키는 과정(최대 우도 추정 등)과 통계적 유의성을 검정하는 방법(Z-test 등)에 대한 상세 내용은 뒤이어 나오는 개별 섹션을 참조하라는 안내입니다.,</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<h3 id="요약-3"><strong>요약</strong></h3>
<p>이 두 문단은 C-SIDE가 <strong>(1) 공변량을 통해 세포의 환경을 정의하고, (2) 각 세포 유형의 고유한 반응(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>)을 개별적으로 추정하며, (3) 유전자별 노이즈를 정밀하게 제어</strong>하여 기존 GLM 방식보다 훨씬 정교하게 공간적 발현 변화를 분석할 수 있음을 이론적으로 설명하고 있습니다.</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="파라미터-계산-순서">7 파라미터 계산 순서</h1>
<p>C-SIDE의 실제 분석 과정은 여러 단계의 계층적 추정을 거치며, 각 단계에서 계산되는 주요 파라미터(그릭 문자 중심)와 순서는 다음과 같습니다.</p>
<h3 id="단계-세포-유형-비율beta-및-플랫폼-효과gamma-추정"><strong>1단계: 세포 유형 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>) 및 플랫폼 효과(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>) 추정</strong></h3>
<p>C-SIDE 분석의 가장 첫 번째 단계는 <strong>RCTD 알고리즘</strong>을 사용하여 각 픽셀의 구성을 파악하는 것입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta_{i,k,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em;
