margin-left: -0.05278em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Cell type proportions):</strong> 각 픽셀(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>) 내에 존재하는 각 세포 유형(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>)의 비율을 먼저 추정합니다.</li>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma_{j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05556em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Platform random effect):</strong> 참조 데이터(scRNA-seq)와 공간 데이터 간의 기술적 차이(플랫폼 효과)를 유전자(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>)별 무작위 효과로 계산하여 보정합니다.</li>
<li>이 두 값은 이후 단계에서 **고정된 입력값(Plug-in estimates)**으로 사용됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-유전자별-과분산sigma_epsilon-추정"><strong>2단계: 유전자별 과분산(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.58056em; vertical-align: -0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>) 추정</strong></h3>
<p>세포 비율이 결정된 후, C-SIDE는 유전자별 노이즈 수준을 측정합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Overdispersion magnitude):</strong> 포아송 모델만으로 설명되지 않는 생물학적/기술적 변이를 반영하는 과분산 파라미터입니다.</li>
<li>가짜 양성(False-positive) 결과를 방지하기 위해 유전자(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>)마다 개별적으로 최대 우도 추정법(MLE)을 통해 계산됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-차등-발현-계수alpha-추정"><strong>3단계: 차등 발현 계수(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>) 추정</strong></h3>
<p>앞서 계산된 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><msub><mi>σ</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta, \gamma, \sigma_\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> 값들을 바탕으로, 연구자가 궁금해하는 핵심 계수를 산출합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{0,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Intercept):</strong> 특정 세포 유형에서 유전자 발현의 기본값(절편)입니다.</li>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{\ell,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (DE effect size):</strong> 연구자가 설정한 공변량(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ell</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord">ℓ</span></span></span></span></span>)(예: 특정 영역, 거리, 상호작용 등)에 따른 유전자 발현의 변화량(Log fold-change)입니다.</li>
<li>이 값은 <strong>2차 신뢰 영역 최적화(Trust-region optimization)</strong> 알고리즘을 통해 수렴될 때까지 반복 계산됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-표준-오차s-계산-및-유의성-검정"><strong>4단계: 표준 오차(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span></span>) 계산 및 유의성 검정</strong></h3>
<p>추정된 계수의 신뢰도를 평가하는 단계입니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>s</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s_{\ell,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Standard error):</strong> 피셔 정보 행렬(Fisher Information Matrix)을 통해 계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>의 표준 오차를 계산합니다.</li>
<li>이후 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Z</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.07153em;">Z</span></span></span></span></span>-test를 통해 유의 확률(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">P</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.13889em;">P</span></span></span></span></span>-value)을 도출하고 최종적으로 차등 발현 유전자(DE gene)를 식별합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="단계-다중-반복-실험-통합-a-및-tau"><strong>5단계: 다중 반복 실험 통합 (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span> 및 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span></span></span></span></span>)</strong></h3>
<p>샘플이 여러 개(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g &gt; 1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.73354em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">&gt;</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.64444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span></span>)인 경우, 이를 통합하여 인구 집단 수준의 결론을 내립니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>τ</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau^2_{\ell,k,j}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.23332em; vertical-align: -0.419216em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.41689em; margin-left: -0.1132em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.419216em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Inter-replicate variance):</strong> 샘플(반복 실험) 간에 발생하는 생물학적/기술적 변이의 크기를 <strong>Method of Moments</strong>로 추정합니다.</li>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>A</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A_{\ell,k,j}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (Population-level DE):</strong> 각 샘플의 추정치(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>)와 변이(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>τ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo separator="true">,</mo><msup><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau^2, s^2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.00855em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>)를 고려한 가중 평균을 통해, 전체 집단을 대표하는 최종 차등 발현 값을 얻게 됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>요약하자면:</strong> <strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta, \gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span> (구성 파악) <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>→</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\rightarrow</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.36687em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mrel">→</span></span></span></span></span> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.58056em; vertical-align: -0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (노이즈 측정) <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>→</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\rightarrow</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.36687em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mrel">→</span></span></span></span></span> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span> (효과 추정) <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>→</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\rightarrow</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.36687em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mrel">→</span></span></span></span></span> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span></span> (신뢰도 검증) <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>→</mo><mi>τ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\rightarrow \tau, A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.36687em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mrel">→</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.87777em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span> (샘플 통합)</strong> 순으로 계산이 진행됩니다.</p>
<hr>
<p>C-SIDE의 계층적 모델에서 **뮤(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)**와 **람다(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>)**는 분석의 핵심적인 중간 계산값과 최종 기대값에 해당하며, 각각 다음과 같은 단계에서 정의되고 얻어집니다.</p>
<h3 id="뮤mu_ikjg-세포-유형별-기대-발현량">1. 뮤(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>): 세포 유형별 기대 발현량</h3>
<p>**뮤(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)**는 특정 세포 유형(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>) 내에서 유전자(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.85396em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span>)가 얼마나 발현될지를 나타내는 값으로, <strong>3단계인 차등 발현 계수(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>) 추정 단계</strong>에서 그 구체적인 값이 결정됩니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>정의 방식:</strong> C-SIDE 모델에서 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>는 우리가 구하고자 하는 계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>와 연구자가 정의한 공변량 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>의 결합으로 정의됩니다 (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mo>∑</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log(\mu_{i,k,j,g}) = \alpha_{0,k,j,g} + \sum x_{i,\ell,g}\alpha_{\ell,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right: 0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.869438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.222222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.222222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mop op-symbol small-op" style="position: relative; top: -5e-06em;">∑</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.0037em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>).</li>
<li><strong>얻는 시점:</strong> 최적화 알고리즘(Trust-region optimization)을 통해 <strong>핵심 파라미터인 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span> 값이 수렴하여 결정되는 순간</strong>, 각 픽셀 위치(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>)에서의 세포별 기대 발현량인 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>가 산출됩니다.</li>
<li><strong>참고:</strong> 분석 초기 단계에서 RCTD가 참조 데이터(scRNA-seq)로부터 얻는 값은 공간적 변화가 고려되지 않은 '평균 프로필’인 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>μ</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\mu}_{k,j}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.19444em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>이며, C-SIDE가 최종적으로 계산해내는 것은 **공간적 맥락이 반영된 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>**입니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="람다lambda_ijg-픽셀-단위-전체-기대-발현율">2. 람다(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>): 픽셀 단위 전체 기대 발현율</h3>
<p>**람다(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>)**는 여러 세포 유형이 섞여 있는 특정 픽셀(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>) 전체에서 해당 유전자가 관측될 최종적인 기대 확률을 의미합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>얻는 단계:</strong> 앞서 계산된 **세포별 발현량(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)**과 **세포 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>)**이 모두 구해진 후, 이들을 합산(Additive mixture)하는 과정에서 얻게 됩니다.</li>
<li><strong>수식적 위치:</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>K</mi></msubsup><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda_{i,j,g} = \sum_{k=1}^K \beta_{i,k,g}\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.28094em; vertical-align: -0.29971em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mop op-symbol small-op" style="position: relative; top: -5e-06em;">∑</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.981231em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.40029em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mrel mtight">=</span><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -3.2029em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.07153em;">K</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.29971em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05278em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (로그 공간에서 플랫폼 효과 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>와 과분산 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">ϵ</span></span></span></span></span>이 더해지기 전의 핵심 혼합값).</li>
<li><strong>의미:</strong> 이렇게 얻어진 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>는 실제 관측 데이터(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>)가 포아송 분포를 통해 생성되는 기초가 됩니다 (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi><mo>∼</mo><mtext>Poisson</mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>N</mi><mi>λ</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y \sim \text{Poisson}(N\lambda)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1em; vertical-align: -0.25em;"></span><span class="mord text"><span class="mord">Poisson</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span></span>). 즉, 모델이 예측하는 **‘이 픽셀의 총 발현 기대치’**라고 볼 수 있습니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="요약하자면">요약하자면</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>(뮤):</strong> <strong>계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 추정하는 단계</strong>에서 공변량과 결합하여 세포 유형별로 먼저 계산됩니다.</li>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>(람다):</strong> 계산된 <strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>값들을 각 세포의 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>)대로 섞어서</strong> 픽셀 단위의 최종 기대치를 구할 때 산출됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<p>이 두 값은 모델이 실제 데이터(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>)를 얼마나 잘 설명하는지 확인하기 위한 **예측값(Predicted counts)**을 생성할 때 중요하게 사용됩니다.</p>
<hr>
<p>수식 초반에 등장하는 **뮤(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)**와 **람다(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>)**는 모델의 계층적 구조를 정의하는 <strong>기대값(Expected values)이자 중간 변수</strong>들입니다.</p>
<p>이 파라미터들이 실제 계산 과정에서 어떻게 다뤄지는지 소스 자료를 바탕으로 더 구체적으로 설명해 드리겠습니다.</p>
<h3 id="모델의-변수로서의-역할-구조적-정의">1. 모델의 변수로서의 역할 (구조적 정의)</h3>
<p>수식 (1)~(3)에서 이들은 관측된 데이터(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>)와 우리가 찾고자 하는 핵심 계수(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>)를 연결하는 가교 역할을 합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>뮤(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu_{i,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>):</strong> 특정 세포 유형(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>)의 <strong>“이론적인” 기대 발현량</strong>을 정의합니다. 이는 공간적 공변량(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span></span>)과 우리가 추정해야 할 계수(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>)의 함수로 설정되어 있습니다.</li>
<li><strong>람다(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>):</strong> 여러 세포가 섞인 <strong>픽셀 전체의 “이론적인” 기대 발현율</strong>입니다. 이는 각 세포의 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>)과 위에서 정의한 세포별 발현량(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)을 합친 값으로 정의됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="실제-계산에서의-처리-optimization-과정">2. 실제 계산에서의 처리 (Optimization 과정)</h3>
<p>C-SIDE는 **최대 우도 추정법(MLE)**을 사용하여 실제 계산을 수행하는데, 이때의 논리는 다음과 같습니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 직접 추정:</strong> 알고리즘은 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>나 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span> 값을 개별적으로 먼저 찾는 것이 아니라, <strong>최종 계수인 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 직접 최적화</strong>합니다.</li>
<li><strong>대입을 통한 단순화:</strong> 수식 (11) 또는 (4)를 보면, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>를 정의하는 로그 선형 수식을 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span> 수식에 대입하여, 전체 식을 <strong>관측 데이터 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>와 미지수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>만의 관계로 변환</strong>합니다.</li>
<li><strong>반복 계산:</strong> 신뢰 영역 최적화(Trust-region optimization) 알고리즘이 돌아가는 동안, <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span> 값이 매 단계 업데이트됩니다. 이때 <strong>계산 내부적으로는 업데이트된 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>에 대응하는 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>와 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span> 값이 계속 변하게 됩니다</strong>.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="최종-결과로서의-확정">3. 최종 결과로서의 확정</h3>
<p>분석이 끝나고 나면 다음과 같은 방식으로 값이 정해집니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>결과 도출:</strong> 최적의 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.69444em;"><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span><span class="" style="top: -3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left: -0.22222em;"><span class="mord">^</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> 값이 결정되면, 이 값을 수식 (3)에 넣어서 각 위치와 세포별 **최종 뮤(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>)**를 얻고, 이를 다시 수식 (2)에 넣어 픽셀별 **최종 람다(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>)**를 얻게 됩니다.</li>
<li><strong>검증에 활용:</strong> 이렇게 얻어진 최종 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span></span>와 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\lambda</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span></span></span></span></span>는 모델이 실제 데이터(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Y</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span></span></span></span></span>)를 얼마나 잘 설명하는지 확인하기 위한 **예측값(Predicted counts)**으로 사용되어 시각화 등에 활용됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>요약하자면</strong>, 질문하신 대로 뮤와 람다는 모델의 논리적 구조를 만드는 **변수(함수)**로 기능하며, 실제 수치적 계산은 이들을 포함한 전체 시스템을 가장 잘 설명하는 <strong>최종 계수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 찾음으로써 한꺼번에 결정</strong>되는 것이라고 이해하시면 완벽합니다.</p>
<hr>
<p>C-SIDE의 실제 계산은 낮은 계층의 데이터 파라미터부터 높은 계층의 통계적 추론 파라미터 순으로 진행됩니다. 각 단계에서 계산되는 주요 파라미터(그릭 문자)와 변수, 그리고 이를 구하는 핵심 수식을 순차적으로 설명해 드립니다.</p>
<h3 id="모델의-기본-구조-기초-수식">0. 모델의 기본 구조 (기초 수식)</h3>
<p>모든 계산은 다음 세 가지 계층적 수식을 기반으로 합니다.</p>
<ul>
