<li><strong>식 (1):</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Y</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>∼</mo><mtext>Poisson</mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>N</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Y_{i,j,g} | \lambda_{i,j,g} \sim \text{Poisson}(N_{i,g}\lambda_{i,j,g})</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.22222em;">Y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.22222em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∼</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.277778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord text"><span class="mord">Poisson</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.10903em;">N</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.10903em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">λ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; <li><strong>식 (2):</strong> <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mi>λ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msubsup><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>K</mi></msubsup><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mi>μ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>ϵ</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log(\lambda_{i,j,g}) = \log(\sum_{k=1}^K \beta_{i,k,g}\mu_{i,k,j,g}) + \gamma_{j,g} + \epsilon_{i,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.03611em; <h3 id="단계-세포-비율beta-및-플랫폼-효과gamma-추정-rctd-단계">1단계: 세포 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>) 및 플랫폼 효과(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.625em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span></span></span></span></span>) 추정 (RCTD 단계)</h3>
<p>C-SIDE 실행 전, RCTD 알고리즘을 통해 기초가 되는 두 값을 먼저 구합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>β</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta_{i,k,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.980548em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05278em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">i</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (세포 유형 비율):</strong> 각 픽셀 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.65952em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span></span>에 포함된 세포 유형 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.69444em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span></span>의 비중입니다.</li>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>γ</mi><mrow><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\gamma_{j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05556em;">γ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.05556em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (플랫폼 효과):</strong> 기술적 차이를 보정하는 유전자별 무작위 효과입니다.</li>
<li><strong>계산 수식:</strong> 공간 데이터를 가상 벌크(Pseudo-bulk) 데이터 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>S</mi><mi>j</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">S_j</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; <h3 id="단계-유전자별-과분산sigma_epsilon-추정-1">2단계: 유전자별 과분산(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.58056em; vertical-align: -0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.151392em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>) 추정</h3>
<p>모델의 노이즈 수준을 결정하기 위해 유전자별 과분산 값을 구합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>σ</mi><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma_{\epsilon,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">σ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.311664em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03588em;">g</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (과분산 크기):</strong> 포아송 분포 이상의 변이를 흡수하는 정규 분포의 표준 편차입니다.</li>
<li><strong>계산 수식:</strong> 다음 확률 함수 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>Q</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Q</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.87777em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">Q</span></span></span></span></span>를 포함하는 우도(Likelihood)를 최대화하여 MLE로 구합니다. <span class="katex--display"><span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>Q</mi><mi>l</mi></msub><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>λ</mi><mo>ˉ</mo></mover><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>≡</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∞</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">∞</mi></msubsup><msub><mi>p</mi><msub><mi>σ</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msub></msub><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>λ</mi><mo>ˉ</mo></mover><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>z</mi></msup></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>z</mi></msup><mover accent="true"><mi>λ</mi><mo>ˉ</mo></mover><msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mi>l</mi></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi><mo stretchy="false">!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Q_l(\bar{\lambda}) \equiv \int_{-\infty}^{\infty} p_{\sigma_\epsilon}(z) e^{-\bar{\lambda}e^z} \frac{(e^z \bar{\lambda})^l}{l!} dz</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.08122em; <h3 id="단계-차등-발현-계수alpha-추정-핵심-최적화">3단계: 차등 발현 계수(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>) 추정 (핵심 최적화)</h3>
<p>가장 중요한 단계로, 앞의 값들을 고정값(Plugin)으로 넣고 최종 목표인 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>를 구합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>α</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{\ell,k,j,g}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.716668em; <h3 id="단계-반복-실험-통합-a-및-tau-메타-분석-단계">5단계: 반복 실험 통합 (<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.68333em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span> 및 <span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>τ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span></span></span></span></span>) (메타 분석 단계)</h3>
<p>여러 샘플의 결과를 하나로 통합하여 인구 집단 수준의 결론을 도출합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>τ</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau^2_{\ell,k,j}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 1.23332em; vertical-align: -0.419216em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.814108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.41689em; margin-left: -0.1132em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span><span class="" style="top: -3.063em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.419216em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (샘플 간 분산):</strong> 반복 실험 간의 생물학적 변이 크기입니다.</li>
<li><strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>A</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ℓ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A_{\ell,k,j}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.969438em; vertical-align: -0.286108em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.336108em;"><span class="" style="top: -2.55em; margin-left: 0em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">ℓ</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct mtight">,</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right: 0.05724em;">j</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.286108em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (인구 집단 수준 DE):</strong> 모든 샘플을 통합한 최종 효과 크기입니다.</li>
<li><strong>계산 수식:</strong>
<ol>
<li><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mover accent="true"><mi>τ</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>V</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>α</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>…</mo><mi>G</mi></mrow></msub><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>−</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>G</mi></mfrac><msubsup><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>G</mi></msubsup><msubsup><mi>s</mi><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\hat{\tau}^2 = \max { 0, V(\hat{\alpha}_{1 \dots G}) - \frac{1}{G} \sum_{g=1}^G s_g^2 }</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.814108em; </ol>
</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>요약:</strong> C-SIDE는 <strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>γ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta, \gamma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; margin-left: -0.03588em; margin-right: 0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height: 2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">ϵ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height: 0.15em;"><span class=""></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (노이즈)</strong> → <strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span> (본체)</strong> → <strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span></span> (신뢰도)</strong> → <strong><span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>τ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tau, A</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.87777em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right: 0.166667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span></span> (통합)</strong> 순으로 수식을 풀어가며 최종 결과를 산출합니다.</p>
<hr>
<h1 id="rctd에서-de">8 RCTD에서 DE</h1>
<p>질문에 대한 답변을 소스 자료를 바탕으로 정리해 드립니다.</p>
<p>결론부터 말씀드리면, <strong>RCTD 자체적으로도 특정 세포 유형의 기대 발현량(Expected expression)을 계산하여 차등 발현(DE)을 탐지하는 기능을 갖추고 있습니다.</strong> 하지만 C-SIDE는 이를 한 단계 더 발전시킨 전용 <strong>모수적 통계 모델</strong>입니다.</p>
<p>상세한 설명은 다음과 같습니다.</p>
<h3 id="rctd의-de-구하는-방식-decompose-방식">1. RCTD의 DE 구하는 방식 (Decompose 방식)</h3>
<p>RCTD 논문에 따르면, RCTD는 각 픽셀의 세포 유형 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>)을 추정한 후, 이를 바탕으로 <strong>각 세포 유형에서 유래한 기대 유전자 발현량</strong>을 계산할 수 있습니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>원리:</strong> 각 픽셀에서 관측된 총 발현량을 해당 픽셀에 섞여 있는 세포들의 비율과 참조 데이터의 프로파일에 비례하여 배분합니다 (conditional expectation).</li>
<li><strong>활용:</strong> 이렇게 계산된 세포 유형별 기대 발현량을 지역 간에 비교하거나 로컬 회귀(Local regression)를 적용하여 공간적으로 변하는 유전자를 찾을 수 있습니다. 소스 자료에서는 이를 <strong>‘decompose’</strong> 방법이라고 명명하여 C-SIDE와 비교하기도 합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="c-side가-필요한-이유-rctd의-한계">2. C-SIDE가 필요한 이유 (RCTD의 한계)</h3>
<p>RCTD의 ‘decompose’ 방식은 픽셀별로 독립적으로 발현량을 할당하기 때문에 몇 가지 한계가 있습니다:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>낮은 통계적 검정력:</strong> 세포 유형이 심하게 섞여 있거나 특정 세포 유형이 희귀한 경우, 개별 픽셀 단위의 추정치는 샘플링 노이즈에 취약합니다.</li>
<li><strong>편향 발생 가능성:</strong> 주변 다른 세포 유형의 발현 시그널이 완전히 분리되지 않고 오염(Contamination)되어 실제 DE가 아닌데도 DE로 오인되는 편향이 발생할 수 있습니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="c-side의-발전된-접근-방식">3. C-SIDE의 발전된 접근 방식</h3>
<p>C-SIDE는 RCTD가 계산한 세포 유형 비율(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.88888em; vertical-align: -0.19444em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.05278em;">β</span></span></span></span></span>)을 <strong>고정된 입력값</strong>으로 활용하지만, 발현량을 분해하는 방식 대신 **로그 선형 모수 모델(Log-linear parametric model)**을 사용하여 직접 DE 계수(<span class="katex--inline"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height: 0.43056em; vertical-align: 0em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right: 0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span></span>)를 추정합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>통합 모델링:</strong> 픽셀들을 개별적으로 보지 않고, 전체 데이터를 공변량(위치, 거리 등)의 함수로 한꺼번에 모델링하여 <strong>통계적 검정력을 대폭 높였습니다</strong>.</li>
<li><strong>강건성:</strong> 다른 세포 유형의 비율 변화나 플랫폼 효과 등을 통계적으로 제어하기 때문에, 단순 분해 방식보다 <strong>훨씬 정밀하고 편향 없는 DE 결과를 도출</strong>합니다.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>요약하자면</strong>, RCTD 자체적으로도 기대 발현량 계산을 통해 DE를 찾을 수 있지만, 이는 초기적인 형태의 분석 방식(‘decompose’)에 가깝습니다. <strong>C-SIDE는 RCTD의 결과를 기반으로 하되, 더 복잡한 통계 모델을 적용하여 세포 혼합 문제 속에서도 훨씬 정확하게 특정 세포 유형의 반응을 찾아내는 전문 도구</strong>라고 이해하시면 됩니다.</p>
